# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

idsempotent dispatch via generation keys.\n17|\n18|State-mapping core (§13.2) — observed Jira state drives ACMS:\n19|\n20|| Observation                                   | ACMS action                       |\n21||-----------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------|\n22|| Ready + AI assignee, no prior start            | import/queue/start (gen 1)       |\n23|| Ready + AI assignee, already queued/running    | reconcile, no duplicate          |\n24|| In Progress + AI assignee + proven gen         | continue/reconcile               |\n25|| In Progress + AI assignee, no proven ready     | Attention; never infer auth      |\n26|| On Hold/Paused/Blocked                         | reversible pause fan-out         |\n27|| Backlog/non-ready after kickoff                | pause; unstarted never start     |\n28|| Reassigned away / unassigned                   | pause immediately; block dispatch|\n29|| In Review                                      | stop launching; checkpoint       |\n30|| Canceled/Won't Do                              | stop generation + descendants    |\n31|| Done/Closed                                    | stop remaining; no fake evidence |\n32|| Unknown/unavailable/read failure               | unknown; inhibit; Attention      |\n33|\n34|Precedence: explicit terminal/cancel > local stop/pause > assignment mismatch\n35|> unknown > non-ready. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n55|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n56|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n57|\n58|\n59|async def _check_worker_busy(db: AsyncSession, agent_id: str, work_item_id: str) -> dict | None:\n60|    \"\"\"One task per worker (plan §3; ADR-0013): refuse a NEW dispatch when the\n61|    target agent already has a non-terminal execution task for a DIFFERENT\n62|    work item. Returns the blocking detail or None.\"\"\"\n63|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n64|        select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n65|        .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.agent_id == agent_id,\n66|               ExecutionTaskRecord.status == \"RUNNING\")\n67|        .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.desc())\n68|        .limit(10)\n69|    )).all()\n70|    for t in rows:\n71|        if t.work_item_id != str, completion_condition: str | None,\n85|    output_artifacts_expected: list[str], model_policy: dict, priority: str,\n86|    callback_base: str, callback_token_hint: str, idempotency_key: \"schema_version\": ASSIGNMENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n92|        \"assignment_id\": assignment_id,\n93|        \"idempotency_key\": idempotency_key,\n94|        \"agent_id\": str(agent_id),\n95| callback_base else None,\n115|        },\n116|        \"callback_token_hint\": callback_token_hint,\n117|        \"created_at\": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),\n118|    }\n119|\n120|\n121|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n122|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n123|\n124|    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n125|    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n126|    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer ---- 2. idempotency: a retry must never double-dispatch ----------------\n174|    dedupe_key = _idempotency_key(work_item_id, active.assignment_id if active else None,\n175|                                  idempotency_key)\n176|    existing = await _find_existing_task(db, dedupe_key)\n177|    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"RUNNING\":\n178|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"duplicate\",\n179|                \"task_id\": existing.task_id, \"external_task_id\": None,\n180|                \"budget_check\": None}\n181|\n182|    # ---- 2a. one-task-per-worker (plan §3; ADR-0013) ------------------------\n183|    busy = await _check_worker_busy(db, str(target_agent), work_item_id)\n184|    if busy is not None:\n185|        await add_event(\n186|            db,\n187|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n188|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n189|            agent_id=target_agent,\n190|            work_key=work.work_key,\n191|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n192|            summary=(f\"409 WORKER_BUSY: {str(target_agent)[:8]} already executes \"\n193|                     f\"{busy['blocking_work_item_id'][:8]} — scheduler must pick \"\n194|                     f\"another idle worker\")[:512],\n195|            metadata={\"worker_busy\": busy, \"work_item_id\": work_item_id,\n196|                      \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key},\n197|        )\n198|        await db.commit()\n199|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"worker_busy\", \"worker_busy\": busy,\n200|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None, \"budget_check\": None}\n201|\n202|    # ---- 2b. JIRA KICKOFF GATE (window-5 §7/§13) BEFORE budget -------------\n203|    # Server-enforced on EVERY dispatch path (UI, API, scheduler, retry,\n204|    # resume, Executive decomposition, product bootstrap — all converge here).\n205|    # Live Jira re-read immediately before the decision (§13.4 step 5).\n206|    from .jira_gate import (ELIGIBLE, JiraGateError, gate_work_item)\n207|\n208|    try:\n209|        gate = await gate_work_item(db, work_item_id, live_check=True)\n210|    except JiraGateError as e:\n211|        raise DispatchError(\"jira-gate-error\", 500, {\"detail\": str(e)[:200]}) from None\n212|    if not gate.eligible:\n213|        await add_event(\n214|            db,\n215|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n216|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n217|            agent_id=target_agent,\n218|            work_key=work.work_key,\n219|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n220|            summary=(f\"NEW execution blocked by Jira kickoff gate on \"\n221|                     f\"{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}: {gate.verdict} — \"\n222|                     f\"{gate.reason[:300]}\")[:512],\n223|            metadata={\"gate_verdict\": gate.to_dict(), \"work_item_id\": work_item_id,\n224|                      \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"gate\": \"jira\"},\n225|        )\n226|        await db.commit()\n227|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": f\"jira_gate_{gate.verdict.lower()}\",\n228|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None,\n229|                \"budget_check\": None, \"jira_gate\": gate.to_dict()}\n230|\n231|    # a2a run id back-filled after send_work\n276|        transport_state=\"DISPATCHING\",\n277|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n278|        idempotency_key=dedupe_key,\n279|        model_policy_json=json.dumps(resolved_policy),\n280|        effective_model=resolved_policy.get(\"preferred_model\"),\n281|        model_resolution_reason=resolved_policy.get(\"resolution_reason\"),\n282|        ttft_deadline_at=ttft_deadline,\n283|    ))\n284|    if task is None:\n285|        raise DispatchError(err or \"task-record-failed\", 500)\n286|\n287|    callback_base = get_settings().callback_base_url or \"\"\n288|    project_ctx: dict | None = None\n289|    if work.project_id:\n290|        from .a2a_models import ProjectRecord\n291|\n292|        proj = await db.get(ProjectRecord, work.project_id)\n293|        if proj is not None:\n294|            project_ctx = {\"id\": proj.project_id, \"name\": proj.name}\n295|    envelope = _build_assignment_envelope(\n296|        work=work,\n297|        agent_id=str(target_agent),\n298|        task_id=task.task_id,\n299|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n300|        session_id=None,  # Never mutated; the\n10|   newest row is the last observation.\n11|\n12|3. ``work_runtime_holds`` — persistent local PAUSE/STOP controls (§13.3).\n13|   A hold survives process restarts and polls; only an explicit operator clear\n14|   (``cleared_at``) removes it. Pausing execution must not disable Jira\n15|   reconciliation — holds are honored by the gate, not by hiding the issue.\n16|\n17|4. ``jira_reconciliation_runs`` — durable ledger for every reconciliation run\n18|   (manual \"Check Jira now\", scheduled poll, bootstrap start request — all go\n19|   through ONE service). what produced the read: dispatch_gate | scheduled_poll | manual_check | bootstrap_request\n62|    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), default=\"manual_check\")\n63|    # read completeness: ok | partial | failed (§13.4 step 3 — partial never advances cursors)\n64|    read_outcome: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default=\"ok\")\n65|    detail_json: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)\n66|\n67|    @staticmethod\n68|    def new_id() -> str:\n69|        return new_id()\n70|\n71|\n72|class WorkRuntimeHoldRecord(Base):\n73|    \"\"\"Persistent local PAUSE/STOP hold (§13.3) — survives restarts and polls.\"\"\"\n74|\n75|    __tablename__ = \"work_runtime_holds\"\n76|\n77|    hold_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)\n78|    work_item_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(\n79|        String(36), ForeignKey(\"work_items.work_item_id\", ondelete=\"CASCADE\"), nullable=False, index=True\n80|    )\n81|    # scheduler | manual_check | ui | api\n86|    source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), default=\"ui\")\n87|    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=_now)\n88|    cleared_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True, index=True)\n89|    cleared_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=True)\n90|    # queued | running | completed | partial | failed\n110|    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default=\"queued\", index=True)\n111|    started_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)\n112|    finished_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)\n113|    # paginated-scan completeness (§13.4 step 3): full | partial | failed\n128|    scan_completeness: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), default=\"full\")\n129|    error_summary: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)\n130|    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=_now, index=True)\n131|\n132|    @staticmethod\n133|    def new_id() -> str:\n134|        return new_id()\n135|\n136|\n137|class JiraSyncSchedulerStateRecord(Base):\n138|    \"\"\"Single-row durable scheduler bookkeeping (§13.6) — UTC timestamps.\"\"\"\n139|\n140|    __tablename__ = \"jira_scheduler_state\"\n141|\n142|    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, default=1)\n143|    last_successful_poll_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)\n144|    last_attempt_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)\n145|    last_run_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=True)\n146|    next_due_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)\n147|    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=_now, onupdate=_now)\n148|\n149|\n150|class JiraIssueLinkRecord(Base):\n151|    \"\"\"(site_id, issue_id) ↔ top-level Work Item authorization (§7.3).\"\"\"\n152|\n153|    __tablename__ = \"jira_issue_links\"\n154|\n155|    link_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), primary_key=True)\n156|    jira_site_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False, default=\"default\")\n157|    jira_issue_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)\n158|    jira_issue_key: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True, index=True)\n159|    jira_url: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)\n160|    # {"content": "1|\"\"\"Jira kickoff eligibility gate (window-5 plan §7/§13).\n2|\n3|The human-facing planning layer is Jira. A Work Item may be dispatched to AI\n4|execution ONLY when:\n5|\n6|  1. it carries a Jira linkage (site + immutable issue id + key), and\n7|  2. the linked issue's CURRENT observed status is a configured READY state\n8|     (default ``TO START`` — project workflow metadata is verified at runtime,\n9|     never assumed globally valid), and\n10|  3. the issue is CURRENTLY assigned to the dedicated AI account\n11|     (startupteamscompany@gmail.com — resolved to a Jira accountId, matched by\n12|     accountId, never by display name or email visibility), and\n13|  4. no local hold (pause/stop) or operator restart authorization is pending.\n14|\n15|Eligibility is NECESSARY but never sufficient: budgets, runtime health, and\n16|local holds still apply after this gate (dispatch order: Jira → budget → …).\n17|\n18|Server-side enforcement: dispatch_service calls :func:`gate_work_item` on every\n19|path (UI, API, scheduler, retry, resume, Executive decomposition, product\n20|bootstrap). Drafts may be saved unlinked; they are visibly non-executable.\n21|\n22|Design notes:\n23|- The gate NEVER mutates Jira. Observation is read-only; transitions are the\n24|  reconciliation service's concern.\n25|- Local holds are durable DB rows — a process restart cannot clear them and a\n26|  poll cannot override them (plan §13.3).\n27|- One Jira issue may authorize multiple ACMS Work Items (§7.3) — descendants\n28|  inherit the authorization; the gate checks the TOP-LEVEL linkage up the\n29|  parent chain as well as the item's own linkage.\n30|- Observation staleness: eligibility is verified at dispatch time with a LIVE\n31|  Jira read when credentials are configured (bounded timeout). If Jira is\n32|  unreachable the gate returns an UNKNOWN verdict — new dispatch is inhibited\n33|  (fail-closed), and running work keeps going (§13.2 last row).\n34|\"\"\"\n35|from __future__ import annotations\n36|\n37|import json\n38|import time\n39|from dataclasses import dataclass, field\n40|from typing import Any\n41|\n42|from sqlalchemy import select\n43|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n44|\n45|from .jira_models import new_id\n46|from .settings import get_settings\n47|from .work_models import WorkItemRecord\n48|\n49|# --- verdict vocabulary (persisted; the UI renders these verbatim) -----------\n50|ELIGIBLE = \"ELIGIBLE\"\n51|NOT_LINKED = \"NOT_LINKED\"\n52|NOT_READY = \"NOT_READY\"\n53|ASSIGNEE_MISMATCH = \"ASSIGNEE_MISMATCH\"\n54|LOCAL_HOLD = \"LOCAL_HOLD\"\n55|UNKNOWN = \"JIRA_UNKNOWN\"\n56|STALE_AUTHORIZATION = \"STALE_AUTHORIZATION\"\n57|\n58|# Local hold kinds (plan §13.3: pause/stop are persistent local controls)\n59|HOLD_PAUSE = \"PAUSE\"\n60|HOLD_STOP = \"STOP\"\n61|\n62|\n63|@dataclass\n64|class GateVerdict:\n65|    eligible: bool\n66|    verdict: str\n67|    reason: str\n68|    observed_status: str | None = None\n69|    observed_assignee_account_id: str | None = None\n70|    checked_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)\n71|    jira_issue_key: str | None = None\n72|    jira_url: str | None = None\n73|    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n74|\n75|    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n76|        return {\n77|            \"eligible\": self.eligible,\n78|            \"verdict\": self.verdict,\n79|            \"reason\": self.reason,\n80|            \"observed_status\": self.observed_status,\n81|            \"observed_assignee_account_id\": self.observed_assignee_account_id,\n82|            \"checked_at\": self.checked_at,\n83|            \"jira_issue_key\": self.jira_issue_key,\n84|            \"jira_url\": self.jira_url,\n85|            **({\"details\": self.details} if self.details else {}),\n86|        }\n87|\n88|\n89|class JiraGateError(Exception):\n90|    \"\"\"Structural gate failure (bad config, malformed linkage).\"\"\"\n91|\n92|\n93|def ready_statuses() -> set[str]:\n94|    \"\"\"Configured READY states (uppercase compare; default {TO START}).\"\"\"\n95|    s = get_settings()\n96|    raw = getattr(s, \"jira_ready_statuses\", \"\") or \"TO START\"\n97|    return {t.strip().upper() for t in raw.split(\",\") if t.strip()}\n98|\n99|\n100|def ai_account_id_setting() -> str:\n101|    \"\"\"Configured dedicated-AI Jira accountId ('' = not pinned).\"\"\"\n102|    return (getattr(get_settings(), \"jira_ai_account_id\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n103|\n104|\n105|def _issue_status_upper(status_name: str | None) -> str:\n106|    return (status_name or \"\").strip().upper()\n107|\n108|\n109|def evaluate_observation(\n110|    *, status_name: str | None, assignee_account_id: str | None,\n111|    has_local_hold: bool, hold_kinds: list[str] | None = None,\n112|) -> GateVerdict:\n113|    \"\"\"Pure mapping of one Jira observation (+local holds) to a GateVerdict.\n114|\n115|    §13.2 precedence: local stop/pause > assignment mismatch > non-ready state;\n116|    an eligible snapshot never overrides budget/hold checks performed later.\n117|    \"\"\"\n118|    s = get_settings()\n119|    ai_id = ai_account_id_setting()\n120|\n121|    if has_local_hold:\n122|        kinds = hold_kinds or []\n123|        kind = HOLD_STOP if HOLD_STOP in kinds else HOLD_PAUSE\n124|        return GateVerdict(\n125|            eligible=False, verdict=LOCAL_HOLD,\n126|            reason=f\"local {kind.lower()} hold persists; an operator must explicitly clear it \"\n127|                   f\"before dispatch (polls cannot override it)\",\n128|            observed_status=status_name, observed_assignee_account_id=assignee_account_id,\n129|        )\n130|\n131|    if ai_id and {"content": "1|from collections.abc import AsyncIterator\n2|\n3|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker, create_async_engine\n4|from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase\n5|from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool\n6|\n7|from .settings import get_settings\n8|\n9|\n10|def async_database_url(url: str) -> str:\n11|    \"\"\"Ensure SQLAlchemy uses the asyncio driver for the target database.\"\"\"\n12|    if url.startswith(\"sqlite:///\"):\n13|        return url.replace(\"sqlite:///\", \"sqlite+aiosqlite:///\", 1)\n14|    if url.startswith(\"postgresql://\"):\n15|        return url.replace(\"postgresql://\", \"postgresql+asyncpg://\", 1)\n16|    return url\n17|\n18|\n19|settings = get_settings()\n20|database_url = async_database_url(settings.database_url)\n21|\n22|# NullPool for SQLite keeps isolated-file test databases from pinning connections\n23|# (and their event loops) across processes; PostgreSQL uses the default async pool.\n24|engine = create_async_engine(\n25|    database_url,\n26|    future=True,\n27|    poolclass=NullPool if database_url.startswith(\"sqlite+aiosqlite\") else None,\n28|)\n29|SessionLocal = async_sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False, class_=AsyncSession)\n30|\n31|\n32|class Base(DeclarativeBase):\n33|    pass\n34|\n35|\n36|async def get_session() -> AsyncIterator[AsyncSession]:\n37|    async with SessionLocal() as session:\n38|        yield session\n39|", "total_lines": 38, "file_size": 1249, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

None = None, timeout: int = 20) -> dict:\n40|    data = json.dumps(body or {}).encode()\n41|    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)\n42|    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n43|    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n44|    for k, v in (headers or {}).items():\n45|        req.add_header(k, v)\n46|    try:\n47|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n48|            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n49|    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n50|        detail = e.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")[:300]\n51|        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n52|    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n53|        raise {"content": "1|# AGENTS.md\n2|\n3|Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.\n4|\n5|## Authority model\n6|\n7|Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.\n8|\n9|Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.\n10|\n11|Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.\n12|\n13|## Read before planning\n14|\n15|For every meaningful change read:\n16|\n17|1. `README.md`\n18|2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`\n19|3. `SPRINT.md`\n20|4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`\n21|5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`\n22|6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs\n23|7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved\n24|8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters\n25|\n26|## Requirement discipline\n27|\n28|Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.\n29|\n30|If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.\n31|\n32|## Future-work provenance\n33|\n34|Every future-work item must identify one of:\n35|\n36|- **Human-Directed**\n37|- **Agent-Discovered**\n38|- **Source-Derived**\n39|\n40|Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.\n41|\n42|## Centralized governance, decentralized execution\n43|\n44|ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.\n45|\n46|A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.\n47|\n48|An agent may have:\n49|\n50|- one primary ACMS work assignment; and\n51|- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.\n52|\n53|## Context authority\n54|\n55|Protected context levels:\n56|\n57|- Organization\n58|- Product\n59|- Project\n60|- Feature\n61|\n62|Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.\n63|\n64|Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.\n65|\n66|If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.\n67|\n68|## Handoffs\n69|\n70|Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:\n71|\n72|1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;\n73|2. what was attempted;\n74|3. what actually changed;\n75|4. what the agent believes the current state is;\n76|5. validation and results;\n77|6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;\n78|7. recommended next action.\n79|\n80|## ADR policy\n81|\n82|Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.\n83|\n84|## Approval timeout policy\n85|\n86|- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.\n87|- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.\n88|- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.\n89|\n90|## Security\n91|\n92|- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.\n93|- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.\n94|- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.\n95|- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.\n96|\n97|## Git workflow\n98|\n99|The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.\n100|\n101|Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.\n102|\n103|Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.\n104|\n105|Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.\n106|", "total_lines": 105, "file_size": 4385, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "100|    async def _poll_agent_heartbeats(self, s, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n101|        \"\"\"Poll-based heartbeat ingestion (plan §16).\"\"\"\n102|        interval = max(15, int(getattr(s, \"heartbeat_interval_seconds\", 60)))\n103|        last = getattr(self, \"_last_heartbeat_poll\", None)\n104|        if last is not None and (now_dt - last).total_seconds() < interval:\n105|            return\n106|        self._last_heartbeat_poll = now_dt\n107|        try:\n108|            from .models import AgentRecord\n109|        except ImportError:\n110|            return\n111|\n112|        async with self._session_factory() as db:\n113|            agents = (await db.scalars(select(AgentRecord))).all()\n114|            # snapshot identity fields BEFORE any commit in the loop: ingest()\n115|            # commits and SQLAlchemy expires all loaded instances, so touching\n116|            # .trust_class afterwards triggers a lazy load in async context\n117|            # (MissingGreenlet class — hit live on prod 2026-10-01).\n118|            agent_ids = [str(a.agent_id) for a in agents\n119|                         if getattr(a, \"trust_class\", None) == \"internal\"]\n120|            await db.commit()  # end the read cleanly\n121|        for agent_id in agent_ids:\n122|            try:\n123|                payload = await self._fetch_worker_status(agent_id)\n124|            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — unreachable: honest absence\n125|                continue\n126|            if payload is None:\n127|                continue\n128|            async with self._session_factory() as db:\n129|                try:\n130|                    await TelemetryService.ingest_heartbeat(db, agent_id, payload)\n131|                    await db.commit()\n132|                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n133|                    await db.rollback()\n134|\n135|    async def _fetch_worker_status(self, agent_id: str):\n136|        \"\"\"Build an acms-heartbeat-v1 snapshot from the worker's Hermes api-server.\n137|        Returns None when the bridge cannot be reached (honest absence).\"\"\"\n138|        import urllib.request\n139|\n140|        from .bridge import HermesBridge, get_bridge_for_agent\n141|        from .telemetry_models import (\n142|            HeartbeatIdentity,\n143|            HeartbeatModel,\n144|            HeartbeatPayload,\n145|            HeartbeatSession,\n146|            HeartbeatUsage,\n147|        )\n148|\n149|        try:\n150|            bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(agent_id)\n151|        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n152|            return None\n153|        if not isinstance(bridge, HermesBridge) or not getattr(bridge, \"_t\", None):\n154|            return None\n155|\n156|        import json\n157|\n158|        def _get(path: str, timeout: int = 8):\n159|            req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{bridge._t.base_url}{path}\")\n160|            req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {bridge._t.api_key}\")\n161|            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n162|                return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n163|\n164|        try:\n165|            health = _get(\"/health/detailed\")\n166|        except Exception:  # never contacted: stays UNKNOWN\n204|\n205|        stale_after = timedelta(seconds=s.heartbeat_stale_seconds)\n206|        reconcile_after = timedelta(seconds=s.stale_reconcile_seconds)\n207|        lc = status.last_contact_at\n208|        if lc.tzinfo is None:\n209|            from datetime import timezone as _tz\n210|            lc = lc.replace(tzinfo=_tz.utc)\n211|        delta = now_dt - lc\n212|\n213|        new_connectivity = status.connectivity\n214|        if delta <= stale_after:\n215|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY\n216|        elif delta >= reconcile_after:\n217|            ok = await self._attempt_reconciliation(db, s, status, now_dt)\n218|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY if ok else CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE\n219|        else:\n220|", "total_lines": 462, "file_size": 21556, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=220 to continue reading (showing 100-219 of 462 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
